00028: def B(a, name, size, center, color):
00029:     a.include(name, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00030: 
00031: def R(a, name, radius, start, end, color):
00032:     a.include(name, rod(radius=radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))
00033: 
00034: def wheel(a, name, center, radius, color, spokes=6):
00035:     w = Asset(name)
00036:     x, y, z = center
00037:     n = 28
00038:     for i in range(n):
00039:         t = 2 * math.pi * i / n
00040:         u = 2 * math.pi * (i + 1) / n
00041:         R(w, "rim_%d" % i, 0.029,
00042:           (x + radius * math.cos(t), y, z + radius * math.sin(t)),
00043:           (x + radius * math.cos(u), y, z + radius * math.sin(u)), color)
00044:     R(w, "hub", 0.085, (x,y-.095,z), (x,y+.095,z), color)
00045:     for i in range(spokes):
00046:         t = 2 * math.pi * i / spokes
00047:         # Slightly swept spokes retain the cast-wheel silhouette.
00048:         pts = [
00049:             (x+.065*math.cos(t), y, z+.065*math.sin(t)),
00050:             (x+radius*.52*math.cos(t+.24), y,
00051:              z+radius*.52*math.sin(t+.24)),
00052:             (x+radius*.96*math.cos(t+.05), y,
00053:              z+radius*.96*math.sin(t+.05))
00054:         ]
00055:         w.include("spoke_%d" % i,
00056:                   sweep_tube(points=pts, radius=.025, color=color))
00057:     a.include(name, w)
00058:     return w

00253: # Containers beneath the bench; cylinders remain open at their visible tops.
00254: def open_container(name,x,y,r,h,color):
00255:     a=Asset(name)
00256:     R(a,"bottom",r,(x,y,.04),(x,y,.08),color)
00257:     n=20
00258:     for i in range(n):
00259:         t=2*math.pi*i/n
00260:         u=2*math.pi*(i+1)/n
00261:         # Flat upright staves form a visibly hollow container.
00262:         mid=(t+u)/2
00263:         side=box(size=(2*r*math.sin(math.pi/n)+.006,.035,h),
00264:                  center=(x+r*math.cos(mid),y+r*math.sin(mid),h/2+.06),
00265:                  color=color)
00266:         side.turn(axis="+z",degrees=math.degrees(mid)+90)
00267:         a.include("side_%d"%i,side)
00268:         R(a,"rim_%d"%i,.018,
00269:           (x+r*math.cos(t),y+r*math.sin(t),h+.06),
00270:           (x+r*math.cos(u),y+r*math.sin(u),h+.06),iron)
00271:     scene.include(name,a)

00301: # Fine wear comes from procedural shading, not additional geometry.

00303:     if not mat.use_nodes:

00305:     bs=mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00306:     if not bs:

00310:     bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value=.83
00311:     nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00312:     links=mat.node_tree.links
00313:     tex=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00314:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value=26
00315:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value=3

00317:     ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position=.18

00321:     links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00322:     links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00323:     bump=nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")

00034: def wheel(a, name, center, radius, color, spokes=6):
00035:     w = Asset(name)
00036:     x, y, z = center
00037:     n = 28
00038:     for i in range(n):
00039:         t = 2 * math.pi * i / n
00040:         u = 2 * math.pi * (i + 1) / n
00041:         R(w, "rim_%d" % i, 0.029,
00042:           (x + radius * math.cos(t), y, z + radius * math.sin(t)),
00043:           (x + radius * math.cos(u), y, z + radius * math.sin(u)), color)
00044:     R(w, "hub", 0.085, (x,y-.095,z), (x,y+.095,z), color)
00045:     for i in range(spokes):
00046:         t = 2 * math.pi * i / spokes
00047:         # Slightly swept spokes retain the cast-wheel silhouette.
00048:         pts = [
00049:             (x+.065*math.cos(t), y, z+.065*math.sin(t)),
00050:             (x+radius*.52*math.cos(t+.24), y,

00052:             (x+radius*.96*math.cos(t+.05), y,
00053:              z+radius*.96*math.sin(t+.05))
00054:         ]
00055:         w.include("spoke_%d" % i,
00056:                   sweep_tube(points=pts, radius=.025, color=color))
00057:     a.include(name, w)
00058:     return w

00030: def B(a, name, size, center, color):
00031:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00032:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00033:     a.include(name, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))

00035: def R(a, name, radius, start, end, color):
00036:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00037:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*start, *end))
00038:     assert sum((u-v)**2 for u,v in zip(start,end)) > 1e-16
00039:     a.include(name, rod(radius=radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))

00042:     assert radius > 0
